| `FC10-C01-U010` | Example / comparison | Chapter I, source L356 | 3)Let $\mathbf { V }$ beanormal algebraic variety,of dimension $\pmb { \eta }$ ,and let W be an irreducible subvariety of $\mathbf { V }$ ,of dimension $n - 1 .$ Let $\mathbf { A } _ { \mathbf { V } / \mathbf { W } }$ be the local ring of $\mathbf { V }$ along $\mathbf { W }$ (i.e.,the set of rational functions $f$ on $\mathbf { V }$ which are defined at leastat one point of W).The normality hypothesis shows that $\mathbf { A } _ { \mathbf { V } / \mathbf { W } }$ is integrallyclosed;the dimension hypothesis shows that it is a one-dimensional local ring;therefore it isa discrete valuation ring (cf. $\ S 2$ prop.3)； its residue fieldis the field of rational functions on $\mathbf { w }$ f ${ \mathfrak { v } } _ { \mathbf { W } }$ denotes the associated valuation,and if $f$ is a rational function on $\mathbf { V }$ the integer $v _ { W } \{ f \}$ is called the “order”of $f$ along $\mathbf { w }$ ;it is the multiplicity of W in the divisor of zeros and poles of $f$ | — |

| `FC10-C11-U003` | Definition / construction | Chapter XI, source L5882 | For $\textbf { G }$ and $\{ \mathbf { G } _ { \Xi } \} _ { \Xi \in \mathbf { X } }$ satisfying the above conditions, we willtransfer to X the terminology of Galois theory.The elements of $\mathbf { x }$ will be called“fields" We write ${ \mathfrak { E } } \subset { \mathfrak { F } }$ if $G _ { \Xi } \supset G _ { \mathfrak { F } }$ we say $\mathbf { \ddot { F / E } }$ isa Galois extension"if $\mathbf { G } _ { \mathfrak { F } }$ is a normal subgroup of $\mathbb { G } _ { \mathtt { E } }$ ,and we call the quotient group $\mathbf { G } _ { \mathbf { E } } / \mathbf { G } _ { \mathbf { F } }$ (also denoted $G _ { f ^ { \prime } f ^ { \prime } } )$ "the Galois group $G \{ \mathbf { F } / \mathbf { E } \} ^ { \prime \prime }$ In the situation of condition a),we call $\mathbf { F }$ "the composite" of the $\boldsymbol { \mathsf { F } } _ { i }$ ,and write $\mathbf { F } = \prod \mathbf { F } _ { i }$ in the situation of c),we write $F ^ { r } = s ( F )$ or simply $\pmb { s F }$ It makes sense to speak of “abelian extensions, solvable extensions",etc.Note that every extension $\mathbf { F } / \mathbf { E }$ is contained in a Galois extension:namely,since $\mathbf { G } _ { \mathtt { F } }$ has finite index in $\mathbf { G _ { E } }$ it only has finitely many conjugates $s . \mathbb { G } _ { F } . s ^ { - 1 }$ and their intersection has the form $\mathbf { G } _ { \mathtt { F } ^ { \prime } }$ ,with $F ^ { \prime } \in X$ ; then $\mathbf { \vec { F } ^ { \prime } } / \mathbf { \vec { E } }$ is Galois and contains $\mathbf { F }$ | `FC10-C11-U001` |
